# Equations des modeles

Page generee par `g2egrid.elements.equations` a partir des modeles eux-memes :
ce sont les expressions que SymPy derive et compile, pas une copie.
Pour la regenerer : `uv run python -m g2egrid.elements.equations >
docs/equations_modeles.md`.

Conventions : `x` les etats (`dx/dt = f`), `z` les variables
algebriques (`0 = g`), `y` les sorties (`y = h`) ; les courants de
port sont injectes dans le bus, en pu base systeme.

## BatterieDCDC

Convertisseur DC/DC et batterie, en parallele sur un bus continu.

- **Parametres** : `Kv`, `Kh`, `Thp`, `Tc`, `Imax`, `Eb`, `Rint`, `Voc0`, `dVoc`, `soc0`, `eps_v`
- **Etats** : `i`, `vf`, `soc`
- **Entrees** : `p_ref`, `v_ref`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `v_dc`
- **Sorties** : `i_dc`, `p`, `soc_out`, `p_bat`

$$
\frac{d\,i}{dt} = \frac{- i + \frac{Kh \cdot \left(- v_{dc} + vf\right) + Kv \cdot \left(- \frac{v_{dc}}{2} + \frac{v_{ref}}{2} + \frac{\sqrt{eps_{v}^{2} + \left(- v_{dc} + v_{ref}\right)^{2}}}{2}\right) + \frac{p_{ref}}{v_{dc}}}{\sqrt[4]{1 + \frac{\left(Kh \cdot \left(- v_{dc} + vf\right) + Kv \cdot \left(- \frac{v_{dc}}{2} + \frac{v_{ref}}{2} + \frac{\sqrt{eps_{v}^{2} + \left(- v_{dc} + v_{ref}\right)^{2}}}{2}\right) + \frac{p_{ref}}{v_{dc}}\right)^{4}}{Imax^{4}}}}}{Tc}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,vf}{dt} = \frac{v_{dc} - vf}{Thp}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,soc}{dt} = \frac{- \frac{Rint \cdot i^{2} \cdot v_{dc}^{2}}{Voc_{0}^{2} \cdot \left(dVoc \cdot \left(soc - soc_{0}\right) + 1\right)^{2}} - i \cdot v_{dc}}{Eb}
$$

$$
i_{dc} = i
$$

$$
p = i \cdot v_{dc}
$$

$$
soc_{out} = soc
$$

$$
p_{bat} = \frac{Rint \cdot i^{2} \cdot v_{dc}^{2}}{Voc_{0}^{2} \cdot \left(dVoc \cdot \left(soc - soc_{0}\right) + 1\right)^{2}} + i \cdot v_{dc}
$$

## ChargeACPref

Charge alternative a puissance constante, pilotee par ``p_ref``.

- **Parametres** : `P0`, `Q0`, `V0`, `Tf`, `mode_bt`, `Vbt`
- **Etats** : `i_d`, `i_q`
- **Entrees** : `p_ref`, `q_ref`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `p`, `q`

$$
\frac{d\,i_{d}}{dt} = \frac{- i_{d} + \frac{\left(1 - mode_{bt}\right) \cdot \left(1.0 \cdot p_{ref} \cdot vd_{t} + 1.0 \cdot q_{ref} \cdot vq_{t}\right)}{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}} + \frac{1.0 \cdot mode_{bt} \cdot \left(p_{ref} \cdot vd_{t} + q_{ref} \cdot vq_{t}\right)}{Vbt^{2}}}{Tf}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,i_{q}}{dt} = \frac{- i_{q} + \frac{\left(1 - mode_{bt}\right) \cdot \left(1.0 \cdot p_{ref} \cdot vq_{t} - 1.0 \cdot q_{ref} \cdot vd_{t}\right)}{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}} + \frac{1.0 \cdot mode_{bt} \cdot \left(p_{ref} \cdot vq_{t} - q_{ref} \cdot vd_{t}\right)}{Vbt^{2}}}{Tf}
$$

$$
id_{t} = - i_{d}
$$

$$
iq_{t} = - i_{q}
$$

$$
p = i_{d} \cdot vd_{t} + i_{q} \cdot vq_{t}
$$

$$
q = i_{d} \cdot vq_{t} - i_{q} \cdot vd_{t}
$$

## GroupeElectrogene

Alternateur synchrone entraine par un moteur diesel via un arbre elastique.

- **Parametres** : `Ra`, `Ll`, `Lad`, `Laq`, `Lfd`, `L1d`, `L1q`, `L2q`, `Rfd`, `R1d`, `R1q`, `R2q`, `Rg`, `H`, `KD`, `mp`, `Tg`, `Tr`, `Ka`, `Ta`, `Ke`, `Te`, `Kfd`, `Tfd`, `K_PSS`, `T_LP`, `T_HP`, `T1n`, `T1d`, `T2n`, `T2d`, `kS`, `wb`, `theta_g0`, `VRmax`, `VRmin`, `mode_e2`, `He`, `KDe`, `Ksh`, `Dsh`, `Rd`, `Kp_g`, `Ki_g`, `Ta_g`, `Td_c`, `Tb_c`, `Fmax`, `Fmin`, `Taw_g`, `mode_fuel`
- **Etats** : `phi_d`, `phi_q`, `phi_fd`, `phi_1d`, `phi_1q`, `phi_2q`, `Dwr`, `Pm`, `Dw1`, `v1`, `v2`, `vpss`, `e1`, `e2`, `efd`, `e3`, `theta`, `Dwe`, `dsh`, `xg`, `fuel`, `xpd`
- **Variables algebriques** : `i_d`, `i_q`, `i_fd`, `i_1d`, `i_1q`, `i_2q`
- **Entrees** : `p_ref`, `v_ref`, `w_set`, `dT_exc`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `P`, `Q`, `V`, `Omega`, `ved`, `veq`, `igd`, `igq`, `Ce`, `theta_out`, `C_arbre`, `w_moteur`, `C_moteur`

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{d}}{dt} = wb \cdot \left(Ra \cdot \left(i_{d} - \frac{vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) + \phi_{q} \cdot \left(Dwr + w_{ref}\right) + vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{q}}{dt} = wb \cdot \left(Ra \cdot \left(i_{q} - \frac{- vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) - \phi_{d} \cdot \left(Dwr + w_{ref}\right) - vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{fd}}{dt} = - Rfd \cdot i_{fd} \cdot wb + \frac{Rfd \cdot efd \cdot wb}{Lad}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{1d}}{dt} = - R1d \cdot i_{1d} \cdot wb
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{1q}}{dt} = - R1q \cdot i_{1q} \cdot wb
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{2q}}{dt} = - R2q \cdot i_{2q} \cdot wb
$$

$$
\frac{d\,Dwr}{dt} = \frac{Dsh \cdot \left(Dwe - Dwr\right) - Dwr \cdot KD + Ksh \cdot dsh + i_{d} \cdot \phi_{q} - i_{q} \cdot \phi_{d}}{2 \cdot H}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,Pm}{dt} = \frac{- Pm - fuel + 2.0 \cdot xpd}{Tb_{c}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,Dw_{1}}{dt} = \frac{- Dw_{1} + Dwr}{T_{LP}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,v_{1}}{dt} = \frac{\frac{K_{PSS} \cdot T_{HP} \cdot \left(- Dw_{1} + Dwr\right)}{T_{LP}} - v_{1}}{T_{HP}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,v_{2}}{dt} = \frac{\frac{T1n \cdot \left(\frac{K_{PSS} \cdot T_{HP} \cdot \left(- Dw_{1} + Dwr\right)}{T_{LP}} - v_{1}\right)}{T_{HP}} + v_{1} - v_{2}}{T1d}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,vpss}{dt} = \frac{v_{2} - vpss + \frac{T2n \cdot \left(\frac{T1n \cdot \left(\frac{K_{PSS} \cdot T_{HP} \cdot \left(- Dw_{1} + Dwr\right)}{T_{LP}} - v_{1}\right)}{T_{HP}} + v_{1} - v_{2}\right)}{T1d}}{T2d}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,e_{1}}{dt} = \frac{- e_{1} + \sqrt{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}}{Tr}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,e_{2}}{dt} = \frac{\left(1 - mode_{e2}^{2}\right) \cdot \left(Ka \cdot \left(- e_{1} - e_{3} + v_{ref} + vpss\right) - e_{2}\right)}{Ta}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,efd}{dt} = \frac{Ke \cdot e_{2} - efd}{Te}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,e_{3}}{dt} = \frac{\frac{Kfd \cdot \left(Ke \cdot e_{2} - efd\right)}{Te} - e_{3}}{Tfd}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\theta}{dt} = Dwr \cdot wb
$$

$$
\frac{d\,Dwe}{dt} = \frac{- Dsh \cdot \left(Dwe - Dwr\right) - Dwe \cdot KDe - Ksh \cdot dsh + \frac{Pm}{Dwe + w_{ref}} + dT_{exc}}{2 \cdot He}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,dsh}{dt} = wb \cdot \left(Dwe - Dwr\right)
$$

$$
\frac{d\,xg}{dt} = Ki_{g} \cdot \left(- Dwe - Rd \cdot \left(fuel - p_{ref}\right) - w_{ref} + w_{set}\right) + \frac{mode_{fuel}^{2} \cdot \left(\frac{Fmax \cdot \left(mode_{fuel}^{2} + mode_{fuel}\right)}{2} + \frac{Fmin \cdot \left(mode_{fuel}^{2} - mode_{fuel}\right)}{2} - Kp_{g} \cdot \left(- Dwe - Rd \cdot \left(fuel - p_{ref}\right) - w_{ref} + w_{set}\right) - xg\right)}{Taw_{g}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,fuel}{dt} = \frac{\left(1 - mode_{fuel}^{2}\right) \cdot \left(Kp_{g} \cdot \left(- Dwe - Rd \cdot \left(fuel - p_{ref}\right) - w_{ref} + w_{set}\right) - fuel + xg\right)}{Ta_{g}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,xpd}{dt} = \frac{2.0 \cdot fuel - 2.0 \cdot xpd}{Td_{c}}
$$

$$
0 = Lad \cdot i_{1d} + Lad \cdot i_{fd} + i_{d} \cdot \left(- Lad - Ll\right) - \phi_{d}
$$

$$
0 = Laq \cdot i_{1q} + Laq \cdot i_{2q} + i_{q} \cdot \left(- Laq - Ll\right) - \phi_{q}
$$

$$
0 = Lad \cdot i_{1d} - Lad \cdot i_{d} + i_{fd} \cdot \left(Lad + Lfd\right) - \phi_{fd}
$$

$$
0 = - Lad \cdot i_{d} + Lad \cdot i_{fd} + i_{1d} \cdot \left(L1d + Lad\right) - \phi_{1d}
$$

$$
0 = Laq \cdot i_{2q} - Laq \cdot i_{q} + i_{1q} \cdot \left(L1q + Laq\right) - \phi_{1q}
$$

$$
0 = Laq \cdot i_{1q} - Laq \cdot i_{q} + i_{2q} \cdot \left(L2q + Laq\right) - \phi_{2q}
$$

$$
id_{t} = kS \cdot \left(\left(i_{d} - \frac{vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} - \left(i_{q} - \frac{- vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
iq_{t} = kS \cdot \left(\left(i_{d} - \frac{vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + \left(i_{q} - \frac{- vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
P = \left(i_{d} - \frac{vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}\right) + \left(i_{q} - \frac{- vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) \cdot \left(- vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
Q = \left(i_{d} - \frac{vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) \cdot \left(- vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}\right) + \left(i_{q} - \frac{- vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) \cdot \left(- vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} - vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
V = \sqrt{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}
$$

$$
\Omega = Dwr + w_{ref}
$$

$$
ved = vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}
$$

$$
veq = - vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}
$$

$$
igd = i_{d} - \frac{vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}
$$

$$
igq = i_{q} - \frac{- vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}
$$

$$
Ce = - i_{d} \cdot \phi_{q} + i_{q} \cdot \phi_{d}
$$

$$
\theta_{out} = \theta
$$

$$
C_{arbre} = Dsh \cdot \left(Dwe - Dwr\right) + Ksh \cdot dsh
$$

$$
w_{moteur} = Dwe + w_{ref}
$$

$$
C_{moteur} = \frac{Pm}{Dwe + w_{ref}} + dT_{exc}
$$

## OnduleurVF

Onduleur de tension a TENSION ET FREQUENCE CONSTANTES, bus continu.

- **Parametres** : `R1_pu`, `L1_pu`, `C_pu`, `R2_pu`, `L2_pu`, `mp`, `nq`, `wf_p`, `wf_q`, `wff`, `Kffi`, `Kffv`, `KpVL`, `KiVL`, `KpCL`, `KiCL`, `kS`, `wb`, `Imax`, `Taw`, `mode_lim`, `Mmax`, `Taw_v`, `mode_vsat`, `Rv_pu`, `Lv_pu`, `zv_dyn`, `kRv`, `XsurR_vi`, `I_vi`, `dI_vi`, `Gdc_pu`, `kVdc`, `bloque`
- **Etats** : `isd`, `isq`, `igd`, `igq`, `ved`, `veq`, `pm`, `theta`, `qm`, `M_VLd`, `M_VLq`, `M_CLd`, `M_CLq`
- **Entrees** : `p_ref`, `q_ref`, `ve_ref`, `w_set`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`, `v_dc`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `i_dc`, `P`, `Q`, `w`, `Vt`, `theta_out`, `vdc_out`

$$
\frac{d\,isd}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(L_{1 pu} \cdot isq \cdot w_{set} - R_{1 pu} \cdot isd - ved + \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{1.0 \cdot 10^{-12} + \frac{\left(Kffv \cdot ved + KpCL \cdot \left(- isd + \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(C_{pu} \cdot ved \cdot wff + Kffi \cdot igq + KpVL \cdot \left(- Lv_{pu} \cdot igd \cdot w_{set} \cdot \left(1 - zv_{dyn}\right) - Rv_{pu} \cdot igq - XsurR_{vi} \cdot isd \cdot kRv \cdot \left(\begin{cases} 0 & \text{for}\: I_{vi} - \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} \geq 0 \\\frac{\left(- I_{vi} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}\right)^{2}}{2 \cdot dI_{vi}} & \text{for}\: dI_{vi} > - I_{vi} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} \\- I_{vi} - \frac{dI_{vi}}{2} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} & \text{otherwise} \end{cases}\right) - isq \cdot kRv \cdot \left(\begin{cases} 0 & \text{for}\: I_{vi} - \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} \geq 0 \\\frac{\left(- I_{vi} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}\right)^{2}}{2 \cdot dI_{vi}} & \text{for}\: dI_{vi} > - I_{vi} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} \\- I_{vi} - \frac{dI_{vi}}{2} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} & \text{otherwise} \end{cases}\right) - veq - \frac{Lv_{pu} \cdot zv_{dyn} \cdot \left(- R_{2 pu} \cdot igq + vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)} + veq - vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)}\right)}{L_{2 pu}}\right) + M_{VLq}\right)^{2} + \left(- C_{pu} \cdot veq \cdot wff + Kffi \cdot igd + KpVL \cdot \left(Lv_{pu} \cdot igq \cdot w_{set} \cdot \left(1 - zv_{dyn}\right) - Rv_{pu} \cdot igd + XsurR_{vi} \cdot isq \cdot kRv \cdot \left(\begin{cases} 0 & \text{for}\: I_{vi} - \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} \geq 0 \\\frac{\left(- I_{vi} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}\right)^{2}}{2 \cdot dI_{vi}} & \text{for}\: dI_{vi} > - I_{vi} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} \\- I_{vi} - \frac{dI_{vi}}{2} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} & \text{otherwise} \end{cases}\right) - isd \cdot kRv \cdot \left(\begin{cases} 0 & \text{for}\: I_{vi} - \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} \geq 0 \\\frac{\left(- I_{vi} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}\right)^{2}}{2 \cdot dI_{vi}} & \text{for}\: dI_{vi} > - I_{vi} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} \\- I_{vi} - \frac{dI_{vi}}{2} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} & \text{otherwise} \end{cases}\right) + nq \cdot \left(q_{ref} - qm\right) + ve_{ref} - ved - \frac{Lv_{pu} \cdot zv_{dyn} \cdot \left(- R_{2 pu} \cdot igd - vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)} + ved - vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)}\right)}{L_{2 pu}}\right) + M_{VLd}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right) \cdot \left(- C_{pu} \cdot veq \cdot wff + Kffi \cdot igd + KpVL \cdot \left(Lv_{pu} \cdot igq \cdot w_{set} \cdot \left(1 - zv_{dyn}\right) - Rv_{pu} \cdot igd + XsurR_{vi} \cdot isq \cdot kRv \cdot \left(\begin{cases} 0 & \text{for}\: I_{vi} - \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} \geq 0 \\\frac{\left(- I_{vi} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}\right)^{2}}{2 \cdot dI_{vi}} & \text{for}\: dI_{vi} > - I_{vi} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} \\- I_{vi} - \frac{dI_{vi}}{2} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} & \text{otherwise} \end{cases}\right) - isd \cdot kRv \cdot \left(\begin{cases} 0 & \text{for}\: I_{vi} - \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} \geq 0 \\\frac{\left(- I_{vi} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}\right)^{2}}{2 \cdot dI_{vi}} & \text{for}\: dI_{vi} > - I_{vi} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} \\- I_{vi} - \frac{dI_{vi}}{2} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} & \text{otherwise} \end{cases}\right) + nq \cdot \left(q_{ref} - qm\right) + ve_{ref} - ved - \frac{Lv_{pu} \cdot zv_{dyn} \cdot \left(- R_{2 pu} \cdot igd - vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)} + ved - vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)}\right)}{L_{2 pu}}\right) + M_{VLd}\right)\right) - L_{1 pu} \cdot isq \cdot wff + M_{CLd}\right)^{2}}{\left(bloque + v_{dc} \cdot \left(1 - bloque\right)\right)^{2}} + \frac{\left(Kffv \cdot veq + KpCL \cdot \left(- isq + \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(C_{pu} \cdot ved \cdot wff + Kffi \cdot igq + KpVL \cdot \left(- Lv_{pu} \cdot igd \cdot w_{set} \cdot \left(1 - zv_{dyn}\right) - Rv_{pu} \cdot igq - XsurR_{vi} \cdot isd \cdot kRv \cdot \left(\begin{cases} 0 & \text{for}\: I_{vi} - \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}} \geq 0 \\\frac{\left(- I_{vi} + \sqrt{isd^{2} + isq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}\right)^{2}}{2 \cdot dI_{vi}} & \text{for}\: dI_{vi} > - P = igd \cdot ved + igq \cdot veq
$$

$$
Q = igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved
$$

$$
w = w_{set}
$$

$$
Vt = \sqrt{ved^{2} + veq^{2}}
$$

$$
\theta_{out} = \theta
$$

$$
vdc_{out} = v_{dc}
$$

## RedresseurDiode

Pont de diodes triphase : modele MOYEN dans le repere dq.

- **Parametres** : `kS`, `kv`, `Rdc`, `Xcom`, `Ld`, `tphi`, `wb`, `eps_v`
- **Etats** : `idc`
- **Entrees** : `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`, `v_dc`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `i_dc`, `p`, `q`, `vr`, `idc_out`

$$
\frac{d\,idc}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- idc \cdot \left(Rdc + \frac{\pi \cdot Xcom}{6}\right) + \frac{kv \cdot \sqrt{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}}{2} - \frac{v_{dc}}{2} + \frac{\sqrt{eps_{v}^{2} + \left(kv \cdot \sqrt{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}} - v_{dc}\right)^{2}}}{2}\right)}{Ld + \frac{\pi^{2} \cdot Xcom}{9}}
$$

$$
id_{t} = - \frac{kS \cdot \left(idc \cdot tphi \cdot vq_{t} \cdot \left(- \frac{\pi \cdot Xcom \cdot idc}{6} + kv \cdot \sqrt{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}\right) + idc \cdot vd_{t} \cdot \left(- \frac{\pi \cdot Xcom \cdot idc}{6} + kv \cdot \sqrt{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}\right)\right)}{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}
$$

$$
iq_{t} = - \frac{kS \cdot \left(- idc \cdot tphi \cdot vd_{t} \cdot \left(- \frac{\pi \cdot Xcom \cdot idc}{6} + kv \cdot \sqrt{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}\right) + idc \cdot vq_{t} \cdot \left(- \frac{\pi \cdot Xcom \cdot idc}{6} + kv \cdot \sqrt{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}\right)\right)}{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}
$$

$$
i_{dc} = idc \cdot kS
$$

$$
p = idc \cdot kS \cdot \left(- \frac{\pi \cdot Xcom \cdot idc}{6} + kv \cdot \sqrt{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}\right)
$$

$$
q = idc \cdot kS \cdot tphi \cdot \left(- \frac{\pi \cdot Xcom \cdot idc}{6} + kv \cdot \sqrt{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}\right)
$$

$$
vr = kv \cdot \sqrt{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}
$$

$$
idc_{out} = idc
$$

## Branch

Branche R-L dynamique entre deux bus, avec rapport de transformation.

- **Parametres** : `R`, `X`, `n`, `wb`
- **Etats** : `i_d`, `i_q`
- **Entrees** : `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_f`, `vq_f`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`
- **Sorties** : `id_f`, `iq_f`, `id_t`, `iq_t`, `p_f`, `q_f`

$$
\frac{d\,i_{d}}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- R \cdot i_{d} + X \cdot i_{q} \cdot w_{ref} - vd_{t} + \frac{vd_{f}}{n}\right)}{X}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,i_{q}}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- R \cdot i_{q} - X \cdot i_{d} \cdot w_{ref} - vq_{t} + \frac{vq_{f}}{n}\right)}{X}
$$

$$
id_{f} = - \frac{i_{d}}{n}
$$

$$
iq_{f} = - \frac{i_{q}}{n}
$$

$$
id_{t} = i_{d}
$$

$$
iq_{t} = i_{q}
$$

$$
p_{f} = - \frac{i_{d} \cdot vd_{f}}{n} - \frac{i_{q} \cdot vq_{f}}{n}
$$

$$
q_{f} = - \frac{i_{d} \cdot vq_{f}}{n} + \frac{i_{q} \cdot vd_{f}}{n}
$$

## Line

Memes equations que `Branch`.

## Transformer

Memes equations que `Branch`.

## Bus

Jeu de barres avec capacite shunt : la tension est un etat.

- **Parametres** : `C`, `wb`
- **Etats** : `vd`, `vq`
- **Entrees** : `isum_d`, `isum_q`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`
- **Sorties** : `vd_o`, `vq_o`, `vmag`

$$
\frac{d\,vd}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(C \cdot vq \cdot w_{ref} + isum_{d}\right)}{C}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,vq}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- C \cdot vd \cdot w_{ref} + isum_{q}\right)}{C}
$$

$$
vd_{o} = vd
$$

$$
vq_{o} = vq
$$

$$
\left|{v}\right| = \sqrt{vd^{2} + vq^{2}}
$$

## BusDC

Jeu de barres CONTINU : une capacite, donc un etat de tension.

- **Parametres** : `C`, `wb`
- **Etats** : `v`
- **Entrees** : `isum`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`
- **Sorties** : `v_o`

$$
\frac{d\,v}{dt} = \frac{isum \cdot wb}{C}
$$

$$
v_{o} = v
$$

## ResistiveBus

Noeud sans capacite : RESISTANCE DE CAUSALITE, comme ``Node.m``.

- **Parametres** : `R`, `wb`
- **Entrees** : `isum_d`, `isum_q`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`
- **Sorties** : `vd_o`, `vq_o`, `vmag`

$$
vd_{o} = R \cdot isum_{d}
$$

$$
vq_{o} = R \cdot isum_{q}
$$

$$
\left|{v}\right| = \sqrt{R^{2} \cdot isum_{d}^{2} + R^{2} \cdot isum_{q}^{2}}
$$

## IdealBus

Noeud SANS capacite ni resistance de causalite : la loi des noeuds exacte.

- **Parametres** : `wb`
- **Variables algebriques** : `vd`, `vq`
- **Entrees** : `isum_d`, `isum_q`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`
- **Sorties** : `vd_o`, `vq_o`, `vmag`

$$
0 = isum_{d}
$$

$$
0 = isum_{q}
$$

$$
vd_{o} = vd
$$

$$
vq_{o} = vq
$$

$$
\left|{v}\right| = \sqrt{vd^{2} + vq^{2}}
$$

## LineDC

Cable continu R-L : le courant est un etat, comme cote alternatif.

- **Parametres** : `R`, `X`, `wb`
- **Etats** : `i`
- **Entrees** : `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `v_f`, `v_t`
- **Sorties** : `i_f`, `i_t`, `p_f`

$$
\frac{d\,i}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- R \cdot i + v_{f} - v_{t}\right)}{X}
$$

$$
i_{f} = - i
$$

$$
i_{t} = i
$$

$$
p_{f} = - i \cdot v_{f}
$$

## SourceDC

Source continue : f.e.m. constante derriere R-L. L'analogue DC de :class:`IB`.

- **Parametres** : `R`, `X`, `kS`, `wb`
- **Etats** : `i`
- **Entrees** : `E_ref`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `v_t`
- **Sorties** : `i_t`, `P`, `Vt`

$$
\frac{d\,i}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(E_{ref} - R \cdot i - v_{t}\right)}{X}
$$

$$
i_{t} = i \cdot kS
$$

$$
P = i \cdot kS \cdot v_{t}
$$

$$
Vt = v_{t}
$$

## ConvertisseurDCDC

Convertisseur DC/DC raccordant une batterie ou un champ PV a un bus continu.

- **Parametres** : `P0`, `Kv`, `vref`, `Tc`
- **Etats** : `i`
- **Entrees** : `dp`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `v_t`
- **Sorties** : `i_t`, `p`

$$
\frac{d\,i}{dt} = \frac{Kv \cdot \left(- v_{t} + vref\right) - i + \frac{P_{0} + dp}{v_{t}}}{Tc}
$$

$$
i_{t} = i
$$

$$
p = i \cdot v_{t}
$$

## ChargeDC_R

Charge continue resistive : ``i = v/R``, sans aucun etat.

- **Parametres** : `R`
- **Entrees** : `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `v_t`
- **Sorties** : `i_t`, `p`

$$
i_{t} = - \frac{v_{t}}{R}
$$

$$
p = \frac{v_{t}^{2}}{R}
$$

## ChargeDC_P

Charge continue a puissance constante, courant filtre au premier ordre.

- **Parametres** : `P0`, `Tf`
- **Etats** : `i`
- **Entrees** : `dp`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `v_t`
- **Sorties** : `i_t`, `p`

$$
\frac{d\,i}{dt} = \frac{- i + \frac{P_{0} + dp}{v_{t}}}{Tf}
$$

$$
i_{t} = - i
$$

$$
p = i \cdot v_{t}
$$

$$
id_{t} = kS \cdot \left(igd \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)} - igq \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
iq_{t} = kS \cdot \left(igd \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)} + igq \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
P = igd \cdot ved + igq \cdot veq
$$

$$
Q = igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved
$$

$$
w = \frac{vdc \cdot w_{ref}}{vdc_{ref}}
$$

$$
Vt = \sqrt{ved^{2} + veq^{2}}

$$
\theta_{out} = \theta

$$

## GFL2DC

GFL a boucle vdc/Q, PORT continu (12 etats) — cf :class:`GFL2`.

- **Parametres** : `R1_pu`, `L1_pu`, `C_pu`, `R2_pu`, `L2_pu`, `KpCL`, `KiCL`, `Kffv`, `wff`, `Kppll`, `Kipll`, `kS`, `wb`, `Imax`, `Taw`, `mode_lim`, `Mmax`, `Taw_v`, `mode_vsat`, `Kpdc`, `Kidc`, `Kpq`, `Kiq`, `Cdc_pu`, `Gdc_pu`, `kVdc`, `bloque`
- **Etats** : `isd`, `isq`, `igd`, `igq`, `ved`, `veq`, `M_d`, `M_q`, `M_CLd`, `M_CLq`, `M_pll`, `theta_pll`
- **Variables algebriques** : `md`, `mq`, `w_pll`
- **Entrees** : `vdc_ref`, `q_ref`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`, `v_dc`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `i_dc`, `P`, `Q`, `w_pll_out`, `Vt`, `vdc_out`, `theta_pll_out`

$$
\frac{d\,isd}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(L_{1 pu} \cdot isq \cdot w_{pll} - R_{1 pu} \cdot isd + md \cdot \left(bloque + v_{dc} \cdot \left(1 - bloque\right)\right) \cdot \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{md^{2} + mq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{vsat} + 1\right) - ved\right)}{L_{1 pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,isq}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- L_{1 pu} \cdot isd \cdot w_{pll} - R_{1 pu} \cdot isq + mq \cdot \left(bloque + v_{dc} \cdot \left(1 - bloque\right)\right) \cdot \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{md^{2} + mq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{vsat} + 1\right) - veq\right)}{L_{1 pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,igd}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(L_{2 pu} \cdot igq \cdot w_{pll} - R_{2 pu} \cdot igd - vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + ved + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)}{L_{2 pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,igq}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- L_{2 pu} \cdot igd \cdot w_{pll} - R_{2 pu} \cdot igq - vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + veq - vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)}{L_{2 pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,ved}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(C_{pu} \cdot veq \cdot w_{pll} - igd + isd\right)}{C_{pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,veq}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- C_{pu} \cdot ved \cdot w_{pll} - igq + isq\right)}{C_{pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{d}}{dt} = Kidc \cdot \left(v_{dc} - vdc_{ref}\right) + \begin{cases} 0 & \text{for}\: Kidc = 0 \\\frac{mode_{lim} \cdot \left(- Kpdc \cdot \left(v_{dc} - vdc_{ref}\right) - M_{d} + \left(Kpdc \cdot \left(v_{dc} - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpdc \cdot \left(v_{dc} - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right)}{Taw} & \text{otherwise} \end{cases}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{q}}{dt} = Kiq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + \frac{mode_{lim} \cdot \left(- Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) - M_{q} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpdc \cdot \left(v_{dc} - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right)}{Taw}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{CLd}}{dt} = KiCL \cdot \left(- isd + \left(Kpdc \cdot \left(v_{dc} - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpdc \cdot \left(v_{dc} - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right) + \frac{mode_{vsat} \cdot \left(bloque + v_{dc} \cdot \left(1 - bloque\right)\right) \cdot \left(md \cdot \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{md^{2} + mq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{vsat} + 1\right) - md\right)}{Taw_{v}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{CLq}}{dt} = KiCL \cdot \left(- isq + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpdc \cdot \left(v_{dc} - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right) + \frac{mode_{vsat} \cdot \left(bloque + v_{dc} \cdot \left(1 - bloque\right)\right) \cdot \left(mq \cdot \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{md^{2} + mq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{vsat} + 1\right) - mq\right)}{Taw_{v}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{pll}}{dt} = Kipll \cdot veq
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\theta_{pll}}{dt} = wb \cdot \left(w_{pll} - w_{ref}\right)
$$

$$
0 = md - \frac{Kffv \cdot ved + KpCL \cdot \left(- isd + \left(Kpdc \cdot \left(v_{dc} - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpdc \cdot \left(v_{dc} - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right) - L_{1 pu} \cdot isq \cdot wff + M_{CLd}}{bloque + v_{dc} \cdot \left(1 - bloque\right)}
$$

$$
0 = mq - \frac{Kffv \cdot veq + KpCL \cdot \left(- isq + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpdc \cdot \left(v_{dc} - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right) + L_{1 pu} \cdot isd \cdot wff + M_{CLq}}{bloque + v_{dc} \cdot \left(1 - bloque\right)}
$$

$$
0 = - Kppll \cdot veq - M_{pll} + w_{pll} - wff
$$

$$
id_{t} = kS \cdot \left(igd \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + igq \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
iq_{t} = kS \cdot \left(- igd \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + igq \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
i_{dc} = - kS \cdot kVdc \cdot \left(1 - bloque\right) \cdot \left(Gdc_{pu} \cdot v_{dc} + isd \cdot md \cdot \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{md^{2} + mq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{vsat} + 1\right) + isq \cdot mq \cdot \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{md^{2} + mq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{vsat} + 1\right)\right)
$$

$$
P = igd \cdot ved + igq \cdot veq
$$

$$
Q = igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved
$$

$$
w_{pll out} = w_{pll}
$$

$$
Vt = \sqrt{ved^{2} + veq^{2}}
$$

$$
vdc_{out} = v_{dc}
$$

$$
\theta_{pll out} = \theta_{pll}
$$

## MMC

MMC, modele moyen a ENERGIE LIBRE, port continu (11 etats).

- **Parametres** : `L1_pu`, `R1_pu`, `Ldc_pu`, `Rdc_pu`, `W0`, `KpCL`, `KiCL`, `Kffv`, `wff`, `Kppll`, `Kipll`, `Kpdc`, `Kidc`, `Kpq`, `Kiq`, `Cdc_pu`, `T_vdc`, `kS`, `kVdc`, `wb`, `Imax`, `Taw`, `mode_lim`, `bloque`
- **Etats** : `isd`, `isq`, `idc`, `W`, `M_d`, `M_q`, `M_CLd`, `M_CLq`, `vdc_f`, `M_pll`, `theta_pll`
- **Entrees** : `vdc_ref`, `q_ref`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`, `v_dc`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `i_dc`, `P`, `Q`, `w_pll_out`, `Vt`, `vdc_out`, `theta_pll_out`, `W_out`

$$
\frac{d\,isd}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(Kffv \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} - vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right) + KpCL \cdot \left(- isd + \left(Kpdc \cdot \left(vdc_{f} - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpdc \cdot \left(vdc_{f} - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(isd \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right) - isq \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} - vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right) - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right) - L_{1 pu} \cdot isq \cdot wff + L_{1 pu} \cdot isq \cdot \left(Kppll \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right) + M_{pll} + wff\right) + M_{CLd} - R_{1 pu} \cdot isd - vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)}{L_{1 pu}}
$$

$$

$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{CLd}}{dt} = KiCL \cdot \left(- isd + \left(Kpdc \cdot \left(vdc_{f} - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpdc \cdot \left(vdc_{f} - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(isd \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right) - isq \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} - vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right) - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right)
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{CLq}}{dt} = KiCL \cdot \left(- isq + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(isd \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right) - isq \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} - vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right) - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpdc \cdot \left(vdc_{f} - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(isd \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right) - isq \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} - vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right) - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right)
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{z}}{dt} = KiZ \cdot \left(KpW \cdot \left(- W + W_{0}\right) + M_{W} - idc + isd \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} - vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right) + isq \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)\right)
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{W}}{dt} = KiW \cdot \left(- W + W_{0}\right)
$$

$$
\frac{d\,vdc_{f}}{dt} = \frac{v_{dc} - vdc_{f}}{T_{vdc}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{pll}}{dt} = Kipll \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\theta_{pll}}{dt} = wb \cdot \left(Kppll \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right) + M_{pll} - w_{ref} + wff\right)
$$

$$
id_{t} = kS \cdot \left(isd \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + isq \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
iq_{t} = kS \cdot \left(- isd \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + isq \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
i_{dc} = - idc \cdot kS \cdot kVdc
$$

$$
P = isd \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} - vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right) + isq \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
Q = isd \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right) - isq \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} - vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
w_{pll out} = Kppll \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right) + M_{pll} + wff
$$

$$
Vt = \sqrt{\left(vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)^{2} + \left(vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} - vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)^{2}}
$$

$$
vdc_{out} = v_{dc}
$$

$$
\theta_{pll out} = \theta_{pll}
$$

$$
W_{out} = W
$$

## RLLoad

Charge R-L serie + echelon de puissance filtre.

- **Parametres** : `R`, `X`, `Tf`, `wb`
- **Etats** : `i_d`, `i_q`, `dp_f`, `dq_f`
- **Entrees** : `dp`, `dq`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `p`, `q`

$$
\frac{d\,i_{d}}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- R \cdot i_{d} + X \cdot i_{q} \cdot w_{ref} + vd_{t}\right)}{X}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,i_{q}}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- R \cdot i_{q} - X \cdot i_{d} \cdot w_{ref} + vq_{t}\right)}{X}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,dp_{f}}{dt} = \frac{dp - dp_{f}}{Tf}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,dq_{f}}{dt} = \frac{dq - dq_{f}}{Tf}
$$

$$
id_{t} = - i_{d} - \frac{dp_{f} \cdot vd_{t} + dq_{f} \cdot vq_{t}}{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}
$$

$$
iq_{t} = - i_{q} - \frac{dp_{f} \cdot vq_{t} - dq_{f} \cdot vd_{t}}{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}
$$

$$
p = vd_{t} \cdot \left(i_{d} + \frac{dp_{f} \cdot vd_{t} + dq_{f} \cdot vq_{t}}{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}\right) + vq_{t} \cdot \left(i_{q} + \frac{dp_{f} \cdot vq_{t} - dq_{f} \cdot vd_{t}}{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}\right)
$$

$$
q = - vd_{t} \cdot \left(i_{q} + \frac{dp_{f} \cdot vq_{t} - dq_{f} \cdot vd_{t}}{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}\right) + vq_{t} \cdot \left(i_{d} + \frac{dp_{f} \cdot vd_{t} + dq_{f} \cdot vq_{t}}{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}\right)
$$

## ZLoad

Charge a impedance constante, fidele a ``symLoad`` de G2Elin V1.

- **Parametres** : `R`, `X`, `wb`
- **Etats** : `i_d`, `i_q`
- **Entrees** : `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `p`, `q`

$$
\frac{d\,i_{d}}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- R \cdot i_{d} + X \cdot i_{q} \cdot w_{ref} + vd_{t}\right)}{X}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,i_{q}}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- R \cdot i_{q} - X \cdot i_{d} \cdot w_{ref} + vq_{t}\right)}{X}
$$

$$
id_{t} = - i_{d}
$$

$$
iq_{t} = - i_{q}
$$

$$
p = i_{d} \cdot vd_{t} + i_{q} \cdot vq_{t}
$$

$$
q = i_{d} \cdot vq_{t} - i_{q} \cdot vd_{t}
$$

## ReactanceShunt

Reactance shunt : une branche R-L vers la terre, a admittance constante.

- **Parametres** : `R`, `X`, `wb`
- **Etats** : `i_d`, `i_q`
- **Entrees** : `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `p`, `q`

$$
\frac{d\,i_{d}}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- R \cdot i_{d} + X \cdot i_{q} \cdot w_{ref} + vd_{t}\right)}{X}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,i_{q}}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- R \cdot i_{q} - X \cdot i_{d} \cdot w_{ref} + vq_{t}\right)}{X}
$$

$$
id_{t} = - i_{d}
$$

$$
iq_{t} = - i_{q}
$$

$$
p = i_{d} \cdot vd_{t} + i_{q} \cdot vq_{t}
$$

$$
q = i_{d} \cdot vq_{t} - i_{q} \cdot vd_{t}
$$

## Defaut

Court-circuit a la terre : une branche R-L d'impedance ``R + jX`` (pu).

- **Parametres** : `R`, `X`, `wb`
- **Etats** : `i_d`, `i_q`
- **Entrees** : `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `p`, `q`

$$
\frac{d\,i_{d}}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- R \cdot i_{d} + X \cdot i_{q} \cdot w_{ref} + vd_{t}\right)}{X}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,i_{q}}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- R \cdot i_{q} - X \cdot i_{d} \cdot w_{ref} + vq_{t}\right)}{X}
$$

$$
id_{t} = - i_{d}
$$

$$
iq_{t} = - i_{q}
$$

$$
p = i_{d} \cdot vd_{t} + i_{q} \cdot vq_{t}
$$

$$
q = i_{d} \cdot vq_{t} - i_{q} \cdot vd_{t}
$$

## DefautDC

Court-circuit POLE A POLE d'un noeud continu : une branche R-L de ``R`` et ``X`` (pu systeme, base du noeud), courant appele ``i`` ::

- **Parametres** : `R`, `X`, `wb`
- **Etats** : `i`
- **Entrees** : `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `v_t`
- **Sorties** : `i_t`, `p`

$$
\frac{d\,i}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- R \cdot i + v_{t}\right)}{X}
$$

$$
i_{t} = - i
$$

$$
p = i \cdot v_{t}
$$

## Repere

Le repere dq global, porte par un bloc a un etat.

- **Parametres** : `wb`
- **Etats** : `theta`
- **Entrees** : `w_in`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`
- **Sorties** : `theta_out`, `w`

$$
\frac{d\,\theta}{dt} = wb \cdot \left(w_{in} - w_{ref}\right)
$$

$$
\theta_{out} = \theta
$$

$$
w = w_{in}
$$

## ILoad

Charge a courant constant : ``|I|`` et facteur de puissance fixes.

- **Parametres** : `P0`, `Q0`, `V0`, `Tf`, `mode_bt`, `Vbt`, `cphi0`, `sphi0`
- **Etats** : `i_d`, `i_q`
- **Entrees** : `dp`, `dq`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `p`, `q`

$$
\frac{d\,i_{d}}{dt} = \frac{- i_{d} + \frac{\left(1 - mode_{bt}\right) \cdot \left(sphi_{0} \cdot \left(Q_{0} + dq\right) + \frac{vd_{t} \cdot \left(P_{0} + dp\right)}{\left(vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}\right)^{0.5}}\right)}{V_{0}} + \frac{mode_{bt} \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \left(P_{0} + dp\right) + vq_{t} \cdot \left(Q_{0} + dq\right)\right)}{V_{0} \cdot Vbt}}{Tf}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,i_{q}}{dt} = \frac{- i_{q} + \frac{\left(1 - mode_{bt}\right) \cdot \left(- cphi_{0} \cdot \left(Q_{0} + dq\right) + \frac{vq_{t} \cdot \left(P_{0} + dp\right)}{\left(vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}\right)^{0.5}}\right)}{V_{0}} + \frac{mode_{bt} \cdot \left(- vd_{t} \cdot \left(Q_{0} + dq\right) + vq_{t} \cdot \left(P_{0} + dp\right)\right)}{V_{0} \cdot Vbt}}{Tf}
$$

$$
id_{t} = - i_{d}
$$

$$
iq_{t} = - i_{q}
$$

$$
p = i_{d} \cdot vd_{t} + i_{q} \cdot vq_{t}
$$

$$
q = i_{d} \cdot vq_{t} - i_{q} \cdot vd_{t}
$$

## PLoad

Charge a puissance constante : ``S = P + jQ`` quelle que soit la tension.

- **Parametres** : `P0`, `Q0`, `V0`, `Tf`, `mode_bt`, `Vbt`
- **Etats** : `i_d`, `i_q`
- **Entrees** : `dp`, `dq`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `p`, `q`

$$
\frac{d\,i_{d}}{dt} = \frac{- i_{d} + \frac{\left(1 - mode_{bt}\right) \cdot \left(1.0 \cdot vd_{t} \cdot \left(P_{0} + dp\right) + 1.0 \cdot vq_{t} \cdot \left(Q_{0} + dq\right)\right)}{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}} + \frac{1.0 \cdot mode_{bt} \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \left(P_{0} + dp\right) + vq_{t} \cdot \left(Q_{0} + dq\right)\right)}{Vbt^{2}}}{Tf}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,i_{q}}{dt} = \frac{- i_{q} + \frac{\left(1 - mode_{bt}\right) \cdot \left(- 1.0 \cdot vd_{t} \cdot \left(Q_{0} + dq\right) + 1.0 \cdot vq_{t} \cdot \left(P_{0} + dp\right)\right)}{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}} + \frac{1.0 \cdot mode_{bt} \cdot \left(- vd_{t} \cdot \left(Q_{0} + dq\right) + vq_{t} \cdot \left(P_{0} + dp\right)\right)}{Vbt^{2}}}{Tf}
$$

$$
id_{t} = - i_{d}
$$

$$
iq_{t} = - i_{q}
$$

$$
p = i_{d} \cdot vd_{t} + i_{q} \cdot vq_{t}
$$

$$
q = i_{d} \cdot vq_{t} - i_{q} \cdot vd_{t}
$$

## IB

Bus infini : f.e.m. constante derriere R-L, avec etat d'angle.

- **Parametres** : `R`, `X`, `kS`, `wb`
- **Etats** : `i_d`, `i_q`, `theta`
- **Entrees** : `E_ref`, `delta_ref`, `w_set`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `P`, `Q`, `Vt`, `theta_out`, `omega`

$$
\frac{d\,i_{d}}{dt} = i_{q} \cdot w_{ref} \cdot wb + \frac{wb \cdot \left(E_{ref} \cdot \cos{\left(\delta_{ref} + \theta - \theta_{g} \right)} - R \cdot i_{d} - vd_{t}\right)}{X}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,i_{q}}{dt} = - i_{d} \cdot w_{ref} \cdot wb + \frac{wb \cdot \left(E_{ref} \cdot \sin{\left(\delta_{ref} + \theta - \theta_{g} \right)} - R \cdot i_{q} - vq_{t}\right)}{X}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\theta}{dt} = wb \cdot \left(- w_{ref} + w_{set}\right)
$$

$$
id_{t} = i_{d} \cdot kS
$$

$$
iq_{t} = i_{q} \cdot kS
$$

$$
P = i_{d} \cdot kS \cdot vd_{t} + i_{q} \cdot kS \cdot vq_{t}
$$

$$
Q = i_{d} \cdot kS \cdot vq_{t} - i_{q} \cdot kS \cdot vd_{t}
$$

$$
Vt = \sqrt{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}
$$

$$
\theta_{out} = \theta
$$

$$
\omega = w_{set}
$$

## SynchronousMachine

Machine synchrone 6 enroulements + AVR 4 etats + PSS + regulateur.

- **Parametres** : `Ra`, `Ll`, `Lad`, `Laq`, `Lfd`, `L1d`, `L1q`, `L2q`, `Rfd`, `R1d`, `R1q`, `R2q`, `Rg`, `H`, `KD`, `mp`, `Tg`, `Tr`, `Ka`, `Ta`, `Ke`, `Te`, `Kfd`, `Tfd`, `K_PSS`, `T_LP`, `T_HP`, `T1n`, `T1d`, `T2n`, `T2d`, `kS`, `wb`, `theta_g0`, `VRmax`, `VRmin`, `mode_e2`
- **Etats** : `phi_d`, `phi_q`, `phi_fd`, `phi_1d`, `phi_1q`, `phi_2q`, `Dwr`, `Pm`, `Dw1`, `v1`, `v2`, `vpss`, `e1`, `e2`, `efd`, `e3`, `theta`
- **Variables algebriques** : `i_d`, `i_q`, `i_fd`, `i_1d`, `i_1q`, `i_2q`
- **Entrees** : `p_ref`, `v_ref`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `P`, `Q`, `V`, `Omega`, `ved`, `veq`, `igd`, `igq`, `Ce`, `theta_out`

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{d}}{dt} = wb \cdot \left(Ra \cdot \left(i_{d} - \frac{vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) + \phi_{q} \cdot \left(Dwr + w_{ref}\right) + vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{q}}{dt} = wb \cdot \left(Ra \cdot \left(i_{q} - \frac{- vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) - \phi_{d} \cdot \left(Dwr + w_{ref}\right) - vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{fd}}{dt} = - Rfd \cdot i_{fd} \cdot wb + \frac{Rfd \cdot efd \cdot wb}{Lad}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{1d}}{dt} = - R1d \cdot i_{1d} \cdot wb
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{1q}}{dt} = - R1q \cdot i_{1q} \cdot wb
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{2q}}{dt} = - R2q \cdot i_{2q} \cdot wb
$$

$$
\frac{d\,Dwr}{dt} = \frac{- Dwr \cdot KD + \frac{Pm}{Dwr + w_{ref}} + i_{d} \cdot \phi_{q} - i_{q} \cdot \phi_{d}}{2 \cdot H}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,Pm}{dt} = \frac{- \frac{100 \cdot Dwr}{mp} - Pm + p_{ref}}{Tg}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,Dw_{1}}{dt} = \frac{- Dw_{1} + Dwr}{T_{LP}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,v_{1}}{dt} = \frac{\frac{K_{PSS} \cdot T_{HP} \cdot \left(- Dw_{1} + Dwr\right)}{T_{LP}} - v_{1}}{T_{HP}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,v_{2}}{dt} = \frac{\frac{T1n \cdot \left(\frac{K_{PSS} \cdot T_{HP} \cdot \left(- Dw_{1} + Dwr\right)}{T_{LP}} - v_{1}\right)}{T_{HP}} + v_{1} - v_{2}}{T1d}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,vpss}{dt} = \frac{v_{2} - vpss + \frac{T2n \cdot \left(\frac{T1n \cdot \left(\frac{K_{PSS} \cdot T_{HP} \cdot \left(- Dw_{1} + Dwr\right)}{T_{LP}} - v_{1}\right)}{T_{HP}} + v_{1} - v_{2}\right)}{T1d}}{T2d}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,e_{1}}{dt} = \frac{- e_{1} + \sqrt{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}}{Tr}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,e_{2}}{dt} = \frac{\left(1 - mode_{e2}^{2}\right) \cdot \left(Ka \cdot \left(- e_{1} - e_{3} + v_{ref} + vpss\right) - e_{2}\right)}{Ta}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,efd}{dt} = \frac{Ke \cdot e_{2} - efd}{Te}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,e_{3}}{dt} = \frac{\frac{Kfd \cdot \left(Ke \cdot e_{2} - efd\right)}{Te} - e_{3}}{Tfd}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\theta}{dt} = Dwr \cdot wb
$$

$$
0 = Lad \cdot i_{1d} + Lad \cdot i_{fd} + i_{d} \cdot \left(- Lad - Ll\right) - \phi_{d}
$$

$$
0 = Laq \cdot i_{1q} + Laq \cdot i_{2q} + i_{q} \cdot \left(- Laq - Ll\right) - \phi_{q}
$$

$$
0 = Lad \cdot i_{1d} - Lad \cdot i_{d} + i_{fd} \cdot \left(Lad + Lfd\right) - \phi_{fd}
$$

$$
0 = - Lad \cdot i_{d} + Lad \cdot i_{fd} + i_{1d} \cdot \left(L1d + Lad\right) - \phi_{1d}
$$

$$
0 = Laq \cdot i_{2q} - Laq \cdot i_{q} + i_{1q} \cdot \left(L1q + Laq\right) - \phi_{1q}
$$

$$
0 = Laq \cdot i_{1q} - Laq \cdot i_{q} + i_{2q} \cdot \left(L2q + Laq\right) - \phi_{2q}
$$

$$
id_{t} = kS \cdot \left(\left(i_{d} - \frac{vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} - \left(i_{q} - \frac{- vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
iq_{t} = kS \cdot \left(\left(i_{d} - \frac{vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + \left(i_{q} - \frac{- vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
P = \left(i_{d} - \frac{vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) \cdot \left(vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}\right) + \left(i_{q} - \frac{- vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) \cdot \left(- vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
Q = \left(i_{d} - \frac{vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) \cdot \left(- vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}\right) + \left(i_{q} - \frac{- vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}\right) \cdot \left(- vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} - vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
V = \sqrt{vd_{t}^{2} + vq_{t}^{2}}
$$

$$
\Omega = Dwr + w_{ref}
$$

$$
ved = vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}
$$

$$
veq = - vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}
$$

$$
igd = i_{d} - \frac{vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}
$$

$$
igq = i_{q} - \frac{- vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)} + vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} + \theta_{g0} \right)}}{Rg}
$$

$$
Ce = - i_{d} \cdot \phi_{q} + i_{q} \cdot \phi_{d}
$$

$$
\theta_{out} = \theta
$$

## SynchronousMachineTR

Machine synchrone AVEC son transfo de raccordement, fidele a ``symSG``.

- **Parametres** : `Ra`, `Ll`, `Lad`, `Laq`, `Lfd`, `L1d`, `L1q`, `L2q`, `Rfd`, `R1d`, `R1q`, `R2q`, `Rg`, `H`, `KD`, `mp`, `Tg`, `Tr`, `Ka`, `Ta`, `Ke`, `Te`, `Kfd`, `Tfd`, `K_PSS`, `T_LP`, `T_HP`, `T1n`, `T1d`, `T2n`, `T2d`, `kS`, `wb`, `theta_g0`, `VRmax`, `VRmin`, `mode_e2`, `Rt`, `Lt`
- **Etats** : `i_gd`, `i_gq`, `phi_d`, `phi_q`, `phi_fd`, `phi_1d`, `phi_1q`, `phi_2q`, `Dwr`, `theta`, `Pm`, `Dw1`, `v1`, `v2`, `vpss`, `e1`, `e2`, `efd`, `e3`
- **Variables algebriques** : `i_d`, `i_q`, `i_fd`, `i_1d`, `i_1q`, `i_2q`, `v_ed`, `v_eq`
- **Entrees** : `p_ref`, `v_ref`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `P`, `Q`, `V`, `Omega`, `ved`, `veq`, `igd`, `igq`, `Ce`, `theta_out`

$$
\frac{d\,i_{gd}}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(Lt \cdot i_{gq} \cdot \left(Dwr + w_{ref}\right) - Rt \cdot i_{gd} + v_{ed} - vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)} - vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)}\right)}{Lt}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,i_{gq}}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- Lt \cdot i_{gd} \cdot \left(Dwr + w_{ref}\right) - Rt \cdot i_{gq} + v_{eq} + vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)} - vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)}\right)}{Lt}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{d}}{dt} = wb \cdot \left(Ra \cdot i_{d} + \phi_{q} \cdot \left(Dwr + w_{ref}\right) + v_{ed}\right)
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{q}}{dt} = wb \cdot \left(Ra \cdot i_{q} - \phi_{d} \cdot \left(Dwr + w_{ref}\right) + v_{eq}\right)
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{fd}}{dt} = - Rfd \cdot i_{fd} \cdot wb + \frac{Rfd \cdot efd \cdot wb}{Lad}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{1d}}{dt} = - R1d \cdot i_{1d} \cdot wb
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{1q}}{dt} = - R1q \cdot i_{1q} \cdot wb
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\phi_{2q}}{dt} = - R2q \cdot i_{2q} \cdot wb
$$

$$
\frac{d\,Dwr}{dt} = \frac{- Dwr \cdot KD + \frac{Pm}{Dwr + w_{ref}} + i_{d} \cdot \phi_{q} - i_{q} \cdot \phi_{d}}{2 \cdot H}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\theta}{dt} = Dwr \cdot wb
$$

$$
\frac{d\,Pm}{dt} = \frac{- \frac{100 \cdot Dwr}{mp} - Pm + p_{ref}}{Tg}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,Dw_{1}}{dt} = \frac{- Dw_{1} + Dwr}{T_{LP}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,v_{1}}{dt} = \frac{\frac{K_{PSS} \cdot T_{HP} \cdot \left(- Dw_{1} + Dwr\right)}{T_{LP}} - v_{1}}{T_{HP}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,v_{2}}{dt} = \frac{\frac{T1n \cdot \left(\frac{K_{PSS} \cdot T_{HP} \cdot \left(- Dw_{1} + Dwr\right)}{T_{LP}} - v_{1}\right)}{T_{HP}} + v_{1} - v_{2}}{T1d}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,vpss}{dt} = \frac{v_{2} - vpss + \frac{T2n \cdot \left(\frac{T1n \cdot \left(\frac{K_{PSS} \cdot T_{HP} \cdot \left(- Dw_{1} + Dwr\right)}{T_{LP}} - v_{1}\right)}{T_{HP}} + v_{1} - v_{2}\right)}{T1d}}{T2d}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,e_{1}}{dt} = \frac{- e_{1} + \sqrt{v_{ed}^{2} + v_{eq}^{2}}}{Tr}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,e_{2}}{dt} = \frac{\left(1 - mode_{e2}^{2}\right) \cdot \left(Ka \cdot \left(- e_{1} - e_{3} + v_{ref} + vpss\right) - e_{2}\right)}{Ta}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,efd}{dt} = \frac{Ke \cdot e_{2} - efd}{Te}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,e_{3}}{dt} = \frac{\frac{Kfd \cdot \left(Ke \cdot e_{2} - efd\right)}{Te} - e_{3}}{Tfd}
$$

$$
0 = Lad \cdot i_{1d} + Lad \cdot i_{fd} + i_{d} \cdot \left(- Lad - Ll\right) - \phi_{d}
$$

$$
0 = Laq \cdot i_{1q} + Laq \cdot i_{2q} + i_{q} \cdot \left(- Laq - Ll\right) - \phi_{q}
$$

$$
0 = Lad \cdot i_{1d} - Lad \cdot i_{d} + i_{fd} \cdot \left(Lad + Lfd\right) - \phi_{fd}
$$

$$
0 = - Lad \cdot i_{d} + Lad \cdot i_{fd} + i_{1d} \cdot \left(L1d + Lad\right) - \phi_{1d}
$$

$$
0 = Laq \cdot i_{2q} - Laq \cdot i_{q} + i_{1q} \cdot \left(L1q + Laq\right) - \phi_{1q}
$$

$$
0 = Laq \cdot i_{1q} - Laq \cdot i_{q} + i_{2q} \cdot \left(L2q + Laq\right) - \phi_{2q}
$$

$$
0 = - Rg \cdot \left(i_{d} - i_{gd}\right) + v_{ed}
$$

$$
0 = - Rg \cdot \left(- i_{gq} + i_{q}\right) + v_{eq}
$$

$$
id_{t} = kS \cdot \left(i_{gd} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)} - i_{gq} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
iq_{t} = kS \cdot \left(i_{gd} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)} + i_{gq} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
P = i_{gd} \cdot v_{ed} + i_{gq} \cdot v_{eq}
$$

$$
Q = i_{gd} \cdot v_{eq} - i_{gq} \cdot v_{ed}
$$

$$
V = \sqrt{v_{ed}^{2} + v_{eq}^{2}}
$$

$$
\Omega = Dwr + w_{ref}
$$

$$
ved = v_{ed}
$$

$$
veq = v_{eq}
$$

$$
igd = i_{gd}
$$

$$
igq = i_{gq}
$$

$$
Ce = - i_{d} \cdot \phi_{q} + i_{q} \cdot \phi_{d}
$$

$$
\theta_{out} = \theta
$$

$$

$$

$$

$$

$$
Q = igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved
$$

$$
w = mp \cdot \left(p_{ref} - pm\right) + w_{ref}
$$

$$
Vt = \sqrt{ved^{2} + veq^{2}}
$$

$$
\theta_{out} = \theta
$$

$$

$$

$$

$$
Q = igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved
$$

$$
w = Dw + w_{ref}
$$

$$
Vt = \sqrt{ved^{2} + veq^{2}}
$$

$$
\theta_{out} = \theta
$$

$$
id_{t} = kS \cdot \left(igd \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)} - igq \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
iq_{t} = kS \cdot \left(igd \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)} + igq \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
P = igd \cdot ved + igq \cdot veq
$$

$$
Q = igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved
$$

$$
w = mp \cdot \left(p_{ref} - pm\right) + w_{ref}
$$

$$
Vt = \sqrt{ved^{2} + veq^{2}}
$$

$$
\theta_{out} = \theta
$$

$$
vdc_{out} = vdc
$$

$$
id_{t} = kS \cdot \left(igd \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)} - igq \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
iq_{t} = kS \cdot \left(igd \cdot \sin{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)} + igq \cdot \cos{\left(\theta - \theta_{g} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
P = igd \cdot ved + igq \cdot veq
$$

$$
Q = igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved
$$

$$
w = Dw + w_{ref}
$$

$$
Vt = \sqrt{ved^{2} + veq^{2}}
$$

$$
\theta_{out} = \theta
$$

$$
vdc_{out} = vdc
$$

## GFL1

GFL a boucles P/Q, bus DC ideal (12 etats) — cf ``GFL1.m``.

- **Parametres** : `R1_pu`, `L1_pu`, `C_pu`, `R2_pu`, `L2_pu`, `KpCL`, `KiCL`, `Kffv`, `wff`, `Kppll`, `Kipll`, `kS`, `wb`, `Imax`, `Taw`, `mode_lim`, `Mmax`, `Taw_v`, `mode_vsat`, `Kpp`, `Kip`, `Kpq`, `Kiq`
- **Etats** : `isd`, `isq`, `igd`, `igq`, `ved`, `veq`, `M_d`, `M_q`, `M_CLd`, `M_CLq`, `M_pll`, `theta_pll`
- **Variables algebriques** : `md`, `mq`, `w_pll`
- **Entrees** : `p_ref`, `q_ref`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `P`, `Q`, `w_pll_out`, `Vt`, `theta_pll_out`

$$
\frac{d\,isd}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(L_{1 pu} \cdot isq \cdot w_{pll} - R_{1 pu} \cdot isd + md \cdot \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{md^{2} + mq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{vsat} + 1\right) - ved\right)}{L_{1 pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,isq}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- L_{1 pu} \cdot isd \cdot w_{pll} - R_{1 pu} \cdot isq + mq \cdot \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{md^{2} + mq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{vsat} + 1\right) - veq\right)}{L_{1 pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,igd}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(L_{2 pu} \cdot igq \cdot w_{pll} - R_{2 pu} \cdot igd - vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + ved + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)}{L_{2 pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,igq}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- L_{2 pu} \cdot igd \cdot w_{pll} - R_{2 pu} \cdot igq - vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + veq - vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)}{L_{2 pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,ved}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(C_{pu} \cdot veq \cdot w_{pll} - igd + isd\right)}{C_{pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,veq}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- C_{pu} \cdot ved \cdot w_{pll} - igq + isq\right)}{C_{pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{d}}{dt} = Kip \cdot \left(- igd \cdot ved - igq \cdot veq + p_{ref}\right) + \frac{mode_{lim} \cdot \left(- Kpp \cdot \left(- igd \cdot ved - igq \cdot veq + p_{ref}\right) - M_{d} + \left(Kpp \cdot \left(- igd \cdot ved - igq \cdot veq + p_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpp \cdot \left(- igd \cdot ved - igq \cdot veq + p_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right)}{Taw}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{q}}{dt} = Kiq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + \frac{mode_{lim} \cdot \left(- Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) - M_{q} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpp \cdot \left(- igd \cdot ved - igq \cdot veq + p_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right)}{Taw}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{CLd}}{dt} = KiCL \cdot \left(- isd + \left(Kpp \cdot \left(- igd \cdot ved - igq \cdot veq + p_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpp \cdot \left(- igd \cdot ved - igq \cdot veq + p_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right) + \frac{mode_{vsat} \cdot \left(md \cdot \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{md^{2} + mq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{vsat} + 1\right) - md\right)}{Taw_{v}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{CLq}}{dt} = KiCL \cdot \left(- isq + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpp \cdot \left(- igd \cdot ved - igq \cdot veq + p_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right) + \frac{mode_{vsat} \cdot \left(mq \cdot \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{md^{2} + mq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{vsat} + 1\right) - mq\right)}{Taw_{v}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{pll}}{dt} = Kipll \cdot veq
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\theta_{pll}}{dt} = wb \cdot \left(w_{pll} - w_{ref}\right)
$$

$$
0 = - 1.0 \cdot Kffv \cdot ved - 1.0 \cdot KpCL \cdot \left(- isd + \left(Kpp \cdot \left(- igd \cdot ved - igq \cdot veq + p_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpp \cdot \left(- igd \cdot ved - igq \cdot veq + p_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right) + 1.0 \cdot L_{1 pu} \cdot isq \cdot wff - 1.0 \cdot M_{CLd} + md
$$

$$
0 = - 1.0 \cdot Kffv \cdot veq - 1.0 \cdot KpCL \cdot \left(- isq + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpp \cdot \left(- igd \cdot ved - igq \cdot veq + p_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right) - 1.0 \cdot L_{1 pu} \cdot isd \cdot wff - 1.0 \cdot M_{CLq} + mq
$$

$$
0 = - Kppll \cdot veq - M_{pll} + w_{pll} - wff
$$

$$
id_{t} = kS \cdot \left(igd \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + igq \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
iq_{t} = kS \cdot \left(- igd \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + igq \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
P = igd \cdot ved + igq \cdot veq
$$

$$
Q = igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved
$$

$$
w_{pll out} = w_{pll}
$$

$$
Vt = \sqrt{ved^{2} + veq^{2}}
$$

$$
\theta_{pll out} = \theta_{pll}
$$

## GFL2

GFL a boucle vdc/Q, bus DC dynamique (14 etats) — cf ``GFL2.m``.

- **Parametres** : `R1_pu`, `L1_pu`, `C_pu`, `R2_pu`, `L2_pu`, `KpCL`, `KiCL`, `Kffv`, `wff`, `Kppll`, `Kipll`, `kS`, `wb`, `Imax`, `Taw`, `mode_lim`, `Mmax`, `Taw_v`, `mode_vsat`, `Kpdc`, `Kidc`, `Kpq`, `Kiq`, `Cdc_pu`, `Gdc_pu`, `Tdc`
- **Etats** : `isd`, `isq`, `igd`, `igq`, `ved`, `veq`, `vdc`, `idc`, `M_d`, `M_q`, `M_CLd`, `M_CLq`, `M_pll`, `theta_pll`
- **Variables algebriques** : `md`, `mq`, `w_pll`
- **Entrees** : `vdc_ref`, `q_ref`, `idc_ref`, `w_ref`, `theta_g`, `vd_t`, `vq_t`
- **Sorties** : `id_t`, `iq_t`, `P`, `Q`, `w_pll_out`, `Vt`, `vdc_out`, `theta_pll_out`

$$
\frac{d\,isd}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(L_{1 pu} \cdot isq \cdot w_{pll} - R_{1 pu} \cdot isd + md \cdot vdc \cdot \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{md^{2} + mq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{vsat} + 1\right) - ved\right)}{L_{1 pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,isq}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- L_{1 pu} \cdot isd \cdot w_{pll} - R_{1 pu} \cdot isq + mq \cdot vdc \cdot \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{md^{2} + mq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{vsat} + 1\right) - veq\right)}{L_{1 pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,igd}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(L_{2 pu} \cdot igq \cdot w_{pll} - R_{2 pu} \cdot igd - vd_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + ved + vq_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)}{L_{2 pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,igq}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- L_{2 pu} \cdot igd \cdot w_{pll} - R_{2 pu} \cdot igq - vd_{t} \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + veq - vq_{t} \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)}{L_{2 pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,ved}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(C_{pu} \cdot veq \cdot w_{pll} - igd + isd\right)}{C_{pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,veq}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- C_{pu} \cdot ved \cdot w_{pll} - igq + isq\right)}{C_{pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,vdc}{dt} = \frac{wb \cdot \left(- Gdc_{pu} \cdot vdc + idc - isd \cdot md \cdot \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{md^{2} + mq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{vsat} + 1\right) - isq \cdot mq \cdot \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{md^{2} + mq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{vsat} + 1\right)\right)}{Cdc_{pu}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,idc}{dt} = \frac{- idc + idc_{ref}}{Tdc}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{d}}{dt} = Kidc \cdot \left(vdc - vdc_{ref}\right) + \begin{cases} 0 & \text{for}\: Kidc = 0 \\\frac{mode_{lim} \cdot \left(- Kpdc \cdot \left(vdc - vdc_{ref}\right) - M_{d} + \left(Kpdc \cdot \left(vdc - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpdc \cdot \left(vdc - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right)}{Taw} & \text{otherwise} \end{cases}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{q}}{dt} = Kiq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + \frac{mode_{lim} \cdot \left(- Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) - M_{q} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpdc \cdot \left(vdc - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right)}{Taw}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{CLd}}{dt} = KiCL \cdot \left(- isd + \left(Kpdc \cdot \left(vdc - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpdc \cdot \left(vdc - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right) + \frac{mode_{vsat} \cdot vdc \cdot \left(md \cdot \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{md^{2} + mq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{vsat} + 1\right) - md\right)}{Taw_{v}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{CLq}}{dt} = KiCL \cdot \left(- isq + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpdc \cdot \left(vdc - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right) + \frac{mode_{vsat} \cdot vdc \cdot \left(mq \cdot \left(mode_{vsat} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Mmax}{\sqrt{md^{2} + mq^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{vsat} + 1\right) - mq\right)}{Taw_{v}}
$$

$$
\frac{d\,M_{pll}}{dt} = Kipll \cdot veq
$$

$$
\frac{d\,\theta_{pll}}{dt} = wb \cdot \left(w_{pll} - w_{ref}\right)
$$

$$
0 = md - \frac{Kffv \cdot ved + KpCL \cdot \left(- isd + \left(Kpdc \cdot \left(vdc - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpdc \cdot \left(vdc - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right) - L_{1 pu} \cdot isq \cdot wff + M_{CLd}}{vdc}
$$

$$
0 = mq - \frac{Kffv \cdot veq + KpCL \cdot \left(- isq + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right) \cdot \left(mode_{lim} \cdot \min\left(1, \frac{Imax}{\sqrt{\left(Kpdc \cdot \left(vdc - vdc_{ref}\right) + M_{d}\right)^{2} + \left(Kpq \cdot \left(igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved - q_{ref}\right) + M_{q}\right)^{2} + 1.0 \cdot 10^{-12}}}\right) - mode_{lim} + 1\right)\right) + L_{1 pu} \cdot isd \cdot wff + M_{CLq}}{vdc}
$$

$$
0 = - Kppll \cdot veq - M_{pll} + w_{pll} - wff
$$

$$
id_{t} = kS \cdot \left(igd \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + igq \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
iq_{t} = kS \cdot \left(- igd \cdot \sin{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)} + igq \cdot \cos{\left(\theta_{g} - \theta_{pll} \right)}\right)
$$

$$
P = igd \cdot ved + igq \cdot veq
$$

$$
Q = igd \cdot veq - igq \cdot ved
$$

$$
w_{pll out} = w_{pll}
$$

$$
Vt = \sqrt{ved^{2} + veq^{2}}
$$

$$
vdc_{out} = vdc
$$

$$
\theta_{pll out} = \theta_{pll}
$$
